"content": "    # Get session information\n    session_age_minutes = None\n    try:\n        if state and hasattr(state, 'session_started_at') and state.session_started_at:\n            from scribe_mcp.utils.time import parse_utc, utcnow\n            start_dt = parse_utc(state.session_started_at)\n            if start_dt:\n                age_delta = utcnow() - start_dt\n                session_age_minutes = age_delta.total_seconds() / 60\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n\n    return NewReminderContext(\n        tool_name=tool_name,\n        project_name=project_name,\n        project_root=str(project_root) if project_root else None,\n        agent_id=agent_id,\n        total_entries=total_entries,\n        minutes_since_log=minutes_since_log,\n        last_log_time=last_log_time,\n        docs_status=docs_status,\n        docs_changed=docs_changed,\n        current_phase=current_phase,\n        session_age_minutes=session_age_minutes,\n        variables=variables or {}\n    )\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Legacy Export API (for direct import compatibility)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n# Export the old classes and functions that might be imported directly\nDEFAULT_SEVERITY = {\"info\": 3, \"warning\": 6, \"urgent\": 9}\nDEFAULT_SUPPRESS_PHASE_TOOLS: Sequence[str] = (\"append_entry\", \"generate_doc_templates\")\n\n# Legacy dataclasses for compatibility\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n\n@dataclass\nclass ReminderConfig:\n    \"\"\"Legacy compatibility dataclass.\"\"\"\n    tone: str\n    severity_weights: Dict[str, int]\n    log_warning_minutes: int\n    log_urgent_minutes: int\n    doc_stale_days: int\n    min_doc_length: int\n    warmup_minutes: int\n    idle_reset_minutes: int\n    suppress_phase_on_tools: Sequence[str]\n\n@dataclass\n", docs_meta.get(\"baseline_hashes\") or {},\n        \"current_hashes\": docs_meta.get(\"current_hashes\") or {},\n        \"last_update_at\": docs_meta.get(\"last_update_at\"),\n        \"update_count\": docs_meta.get(\"update_count\"),\n    }\n\n\nasync def _count_log_entries(log_path) -> int:\n    try:\n        lines = await read_all_lines(log_path)\n    except Exception:\n        return 0\n    count = 0\n    for line in lines:\n        if parse_log_line(line):\n            count += 1\n    return count\n\n\nasync def _compute_log_counts(project: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    counts: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n    logs = log_config_module.load_log_config()\n    for log_type in sorted(logs.keys()):\n        try:\n            path, _definition = resolve_log_definition(project, log_type)\n            if not path.exists():\n                counts[log_type] = 0\n                continue\n            counts[log_type] = await _count_log_entries(path)\n        except Exception:\n            continue\n    return counts\n\n\nasync def _read_recent_progress_entries(progress_log_path: str, limit: int = 5) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Read last N entries from progress log.\n\n    Args:\n        progress_log_path: Path to PROGRESS_LOG.md file\n        limit: Maximum number of recent entries to return (1-5)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of entry dicts with timestamp, emoji, agent, message\n        (COMPLETE messages - NO truncation!)\n    \"\"\"\n    if not progress_log_path or not Path(progress_log_path).exists():\n        return []\n\n    try:\n        # Read the log file\n        with open(progress_log_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f:\n            lines = f.readlines()\n\n        # Parse entries (lines starting with '[')\n        entries = []\n        for line in lines:\n            line = line.strip()\n            if not line.startswith('['):\n                continue\n\n            # Parse entry format: [emoji] [timestamp] [Agent: name] [Project: name] message\n            # Example: [\u2139\ufe0f] [2026-01-03 09:53:42 UTC] [Agent: Orchestrator] [Project: xyz] Message here\n\n            try:\n                parts = line.split('] ', 4)  # Split on '] ' up to 5 parts\n                if len(parts) < 5:\n                    continue\n\n                emoji = parts[0].strip('[')\n                timestamp = parts[1].strip('[')\n                agent_part = parts[2].strip('[')  # \"Agent: name\"\n                # Skip project part (parts[3])\n                message = parts[4]\n\n                # Extract agent name\n                agent = agent_part.replace('Agent: ', '') if 'Agent:' in agent_part else 'unknown'\n\n                entries.append({\n                    \"emoji\": emoji,\n                    \"timestamp\": timestamp,\n                    \"agent\": agent,\n                    \"message\": message  # COMPLETE message - NO truncation!\n                })\n            except:\n                continue\n\n        # Return last N entries\n        return entries[-limit:] if len(entries) > limit else entries\n\n    except Exception:\n        return []\n\n\nasync def _gather_doc_info(project: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Gather document information for a project.\n\n    Returns dict with architecture/phase_plan/checklist/progress info.\n    \"\"\"\n    from utils.response import default_formatter\n\n    progress_log = project.get('progress_log', '')\n    if not progress_log or not Path(progress_log).exists():\n        return {}\n\n    dev_plan_dir = Path(progress_log).parent\n    result = {}\n\n    # Check standard documents\n    arch_file = dev_plan_dir /     }\n\n    phase_file = dev_plan_dir / \"PHASE_PLAN.md\"\n    if phase_file.exists():\n        result[\"phase_plan\"] = {\n            \"exists\": True,\n            \"lines\": \"exists\": True,\n            \"lines\": default_formatter._get_doc_line_count(checklist_file)\n        }\n\n    # Progress log - count entries\n    prog_file = Path(progress_log)\n    if prog_file.exists():\n        try:\n            with open(prog_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f:\n                content = f.read()\n                entry_count = sum(1 for line in content.split('\\n') if line.strip().startswith('['))\n            result[\"progress\"] = {\"exists\": True, \"entries\": entry_count}\n        except:\n            result[\"progress\"] = {\"exists\": "chunk_index": 0, "content": "\"\"\"Reusable mixin for logging-focused MCP tools.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, Optional\n\nfrom scribe_mcp.utils.response import default_formatter\nfrom scribe_mcp.shared.project_registry import ProjectRegistry\n\nfrom .logging_utils import (\n    LoggingContext,\n    ProjectResolutionError,\n    ensure_metadata_requirements,\n    resolve_logging_context,\n)\n\n\nclass LoggingToolMixin:\n    \"\"\"Mixin providing convenience helpers for logging-oriented tools.\n\n    The mixin assumes the concrete tool already provides a ``server_module``\n    attribute (as exposed by ``BaseTool``) so it can tap into the shared state\n    manager and reminder infrastructure.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    server_module: Any  # Provided by BaseTool subclasses.\n\n    async def prepare_context(\n        self,\n        *,\n        tool_name: str,\n        agent_id: Optional[str] = None,\n        explicit_project: Optional[str] = None,\n        require_project: bool = True,\n        state_snapshot: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\n        reminder_variables: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\n    ) -> LoggingContext:\n        \"\"\"Resolve project, reminders, and state snapshot for a logging tool.\"\"\"\n        if not getattr(self, \"server_module\", None):\n            raise RuntimeError(\"LoggingToolMixin requires 'server_module' attribute.\")\n\n        return await resolve_logging_context(\n            tool_name=tool_name,\n            server_module=self.server_module,\n            agent_id=agent_id,\n            explicit_project=explicit_project,\n            require_project=require_project,\n            state_snapshot=state_snapshot,\n            reminder_variables=reminder_variables,\n        )\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def apply_context_payload(\n        response: Dict[str, Any],\n        context: LoggingContext,\n    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Attach reminders and recent projects to a tool response.\"\"\"\n        # Ensure context appears at the end for readability.\n        recent_projects = response.pop(\"recent_projects\", list(context.recent_projects))\n        reminders = response.pop(\"reminders\", list(context.reminders))\n        response[\"recent_projects\"] = recent_projects\n        response[\"reminders\"] = reminders\n        return response\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def error_response(\n        message: str,\n        *,\n        suggestion: Optional[str] = None,\n        context: Optional[LoggingContext] = None,\n        extra: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\n    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Generate a standardized error payload.\"\"\"\n        payload: Dict[str, Any] = {\"ok\": False, \"error\": message}\n        if suggestion:\n            payload[\"suggestion\"] = suggestion\n        if extra:\n            payload.update(extra)\n        if context:\n            LoggingToolMixin.apply_context_payload(payload, context)\n        return payload\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def success_with_entries(\n        *,\n        entries,\n        context: LoggingContext,\n        compact: bool = False,\n        fields: Optional[list[str]] = None,\n        include_metadata: bool = True,\n        pagination: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\n        extra_data: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\n    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Format entries using the shared default formatter and attach context data.\"\"\"\n        response = default_formatter.format_response(\n            entries=entries,\n            compact=compact,\n            fields=fields,\n            include_metadata=include_metadata,\n            pagination=pagination,\n            extra_data=extra_data or {},\n        )\n        return LoggingToolMixin.apply_context_payload(response, context)\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def validate_metadata_requirements(\n        log_definition: Dict[str, Any],\n        meta_payload: Dict[str, Any],\n    ) -> Optional[str]:\n        \"\"\"Delegate to the shared metadata requirement validator.\"\"\"\n        return ensure_metadata_requirements(log_definition, meta_payload)\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def translate_project_error(\n        error: ProjectResolutionError,\n    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Convert a ProjectResolutionError into a tool-friendly response dict.\"\"\"\n        payload = {\"ok\": False, \"error\": str(error)}\n        if error.recent_projects:\n            payload[\"recent_projects\"] = list(error.recent_projects)\n\n        # Best-effort hint: show last known project + recency without auto-restoring.\n        try:\n            registry = ProjectRegistry()\n            last_known = registry.get_last_known_project(candidates=list(error.recent_projects))\n            if last_known and last_known.last_access_at:\n                minutes_ago = int(\n                    max(\n                        0.0,\n                        (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - last_known.last_access_at).total_seconds() / 60.0,\n                    )\n                )\n                payload[\"last_known_project\"] = last_known.project_name\n                payload[\"last_known_project_minutes_ago\"] = minutes_ago\n                payload[\"last_known_project_last_access_at\"] = last_known.last_access_at.isoformat()\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n        return payload\n", "line_end": 139, "line_start": 1}], "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, [ℹ️] [2026-01-03 19:20:34 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunks": [{"byte_end": 7984, "byte_start": 0, "chunk_index": 0, "content": "\n# \ud83c\udfd7\ufe0f Architecture Guide \u2014 scribe_tool_output_refinement\n**Author:** Scribe\n**Version:** Draft v0.1\n**Status:** Draft\n**Last Updated:** 2026-01-02 13:47:01 UTC\n\n> Architecture guide for scribe_tool_output_refinement.\n\n---\n## 1. Problem Statement\n<!-- ID: problem_statement -->\n## 1. Problem Statement\n<!-- ID: problem_statement -->\n- **Context:** Scribe MCP tools return JSON-wrapped outputs that are difficult for AI agents to read and use effectively. The `read_file` tool exemplifies this: content is buried in nested structures with escaped newlines, no line numbers, and metadata mixed with content. Agents avoid using these tools despite their audit capabilities.\n- **Goals:**\n  - Make ALL Scribe MCP tool outputs agent-friendly and highly readable\n  - Preserve full audit trail (sha256, provenance, timestamps, agent identity)\n  - Start with `read_file` as the priority tool, then expand to all tools\n  - Create output formats that agents WANT to use\n  - Maintain backward compatibility for existing integrations\n- **Non-Goals:**\n  - Breaking existing API contracts\n  - Removing audit/provenance capabilities\n  - Creating entirely new tools (we refine existing ones)\n- **Success Metrics:**\n  - Agents prefer Scribe tools over raw file operations\n  - Output is readable at a glance (line numbers, actual line breaks)\n  - Audit data is accessible but not obstructive\n  - All existing tests continue to pass\n<!-- ID: requirements_constraints -->\n## 2. Requirements & Constraints\n<!-- ID: requirements_constraints -->\n\n### Functional Requirements\n\n**FR1: Multi-Format Output Support**\n- All tools MUST support three output formats: `readable`, `structured`, `compact`\n- Format selection via optional `format` parameter (default: `structured` for backward compatibility)\n- Each format serves distinct use case:\n  - `readable`: Agent-friendly with line numbers, visual separators, actual line breaks\n  - `structured`: Current JSON format for programmatic consumers (backward compatible)\n  - `compact`: Minimal output for token-constrained scenarios\n\n**FR2: Readable Format Specification**\n- Content MUST be line-numbered using cat -n style: `  1\u2192content`, `  2\u2192content`\n- Line numbers right-aligned with arrow separator for scannability\n- Actual line breaks (NOT escaped `\\n`)\n- Metadata in separate collapsible section with visual boundaries\n- ASCII art separators for clear visual hierarchy\n\n**FR3: Priority Tool Coverage**\n1. `read_file` - File content with provenance (HIGHEST PRIORITY)\n2. `append_entry` - Log confirmation\n3. `read_recent` - Recent log entries\n4. `query_entries` - Search results\n5. `list_projects` - Project listing\n6. All remaining 9 tools\n\n**FR4: Metadata Preservation**\n- All audit data MUST be preserved: sha256, timestamps, provenance, agent identity\n- Metadata placed in separate section (not mixed with content)\n- Collapsible metadata blocks using ASCII separators\n- Full audit trail accessible in all formats\n\n### Non-Functional Requirements\n\n**NFR1: Backward Compatibility**\n- Default format MUST be `structured` (current behavior)\n- Existing tool responses MUST remain unchanged when format parameter not specified\n- All existing tests MUST pass without modification\n- No breaking changes to tool APIs\n\n**NFR2: Performance**\n- Output formatting MUST NOT degrade performance below existing baselines\n- `readable` format generation \u2264 5ms overhead per tool call\n- Memory overhead \u2264 2x current usage (for formatted strings)\n- Existing performance test suite MUST pass\n\n**NFR3: Maintainability**\n- Extend existing `utils/response.py` - NO new modules\n- Single source of truth for formatting logic\n- Consistent API across all tools\n- Clear documentation for format implementations\n\n### Hard Constraints\n\n**C1: Audit Trail Completeness**\n- SHA256 hashes for all file operations\n- Timestamp precision to microsecond\n- Agent identity tracking\n- Execution context provenance\n- All fields currently in responses MUST remain accessible\n\n**C2: Error Reporting Structure**\n- `ok` boolean for success/failure\n- `error` field with descriptive messages\n- Error format consistent across all three output modes\n- Stack traces preserved in structured format\n\n**C3: Reminders System Integration**\n- Reminder arrays MUST be included in all responses\n- Reminder format preserved for downstream processing\n- Context-aware reminders based on project state\n\n**C4: Storage Backend Neutrality**\n- Formatting layer MUST NOT depend on SQLite vs PostgreSQL\n- Work with abstract storage interface only\n- No database-specific output formatting\n\n**C5: MCP Protocol Compliance**\n- All tools remain valid MCP tool definitions\n- Parameter types compatible with MCP type system\n- Response structure parseable by MCP clients\n\n### Assumptions\n\n- Filesystem read/write access for output operations\n- Python 3.10+ runtime with typing support\n- UTF-8 encoding for all text content\n- Terminal width \u2265 80 characters for readable format optimal display\n\n### Risks & Mitigations\n\n**R1: Performance Degradation**\n- Risk: String formatting overhead for readable mode\n- Mitigation: Lazy formatting (only when format='readable'), optimize string operations, performance benchmarks in CI\n\n**R2: Breaking Existing Consumers**\n- Risk: Unknown external dependencies on current response structure\n- Mitigation: Default format='structured', comprehensive testing, version documentation\n\n**R3: Format Inconsistency**\n- Risk: Different tools implement readable format differently\n- Mitigation: Centralized ResponseFormatter, comprehensive format specification, code review checklist\n\n**R4: Metadata Loss**\n- Risk: Readable format might accidentally omit critical audit data\n- Mitigation: Metadata preservation tests, audit trail validation, explicit metadata section in readable output\n\n**R5: Maintenance Burden**\n- Risk: Three formats = 3x maintenance cost\n- Mitigation: Shared formatting logic in ResponseFormatter, automated testing for all formats, format specification document\n<!-- ID: architecture_overview -->\n## 3. Architecture Overview\n<!-- ID: architecture_overview -->\n- **Solution Summary:** Introduce a unified output formatting layer for all Scribe MCP tools that separates audit metadata from readable content, with configurable format options.\n- **Tools In Scope (Priority Order):**\n  1. `read_file` - File reading with provenance (PRIORITY)\n  2. `append_entry` - Log entry creation and confirmation\n  3. `read_recent` - Recent log retrieval\n  4. `query_entries` - Log search and filtering\n  5. `manage_docs` - Document management operations\n  6. `list_projects` - Project discovery\n  7. `get_project` - Project context\n  8. `set_project` - Project initialization\n  9. `rotate_log` - Log rotation\n  10. `scribe_doctor` - Diagnostics\n- **Proposed Output Structure (To Be Refined by Research):**\n  ```\n  \u250c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510\n  \u2502 METADATA BLOCK (collapsible/optional)   \u2502\n  \u2502 - sha256, provenance, timestamps        \u2502\n  \u251c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2524\n  \u2502 CONTENT BLOCK (primary, readable)       \u2502\n  \u2502 - Line numbers, actual formatting       \u2502\n  \u2502 - Clean, scannable output               \u2502\n  \u2514\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2518\n  ```\n- **Format Options (To Be Validated):**\n  - `readable` - Human/agent-friendly (default)\n  - `structured` - Full JSON for programmatic use\n  - `compact` - Minimal output for quick checks\n- **Key Constraints:**\n  - Must not break existing API consumers\n  - Audit trail must remain complete\n  - Performance must not degrade significantly\n<!-- ID: detailed_design -->\n## 4. Detailed Design\n<!-- ID: detailed_design -->\n\n### 4.1 ResponseFormatter Extension Architecture\n\n**Core Design Pattern: Extend Existing Infrastructure**\n\n", "frontmatter_stripped": true, "line_end": 185, "line_start": 1, "original_byte_end": 8320, "original_byte_start": 0, "original_line_end": 200, "original_line_start": 1}], "frontmatter": {"category": "engineering", "created_by": "Corta Labs", "doc_type": "architecture", "id": "scribe_tool_output_refinement-architecture", "last_updated": "2026-01-02", "maintained_by": "Corta Labs", "owners": [], "related_docs": [], "status": "draft", "summary": "", "tags": [], "title": "\ud83c\udfd7\ufe0f Architecture Guide \u2014 scribe_tool_output_refinement", "version": "0.1"}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 336, "frontmatter_line_count": 15, "frontmatter_raw": "---\nid: scribe_tool_output_refinement-architecture\ntitle: \"\\U0001F3D7\\uFE0F Architecture Guide \\u2014 scribe_tool_output_refinement\"\ndoc_type: architecture\ncategory: engineering\nstatus: draft\nversion: '0.1'\nlast_updated: '2026-01-02'\nmaintained_by: Corta Labs\ncreated_by: Corta Labs\nowners: []\nrelated_docs: []\ntags: []\nsummary: ''\n---\n", "has_frontmatter": true, "mode": "chunk", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/.scribe/docs/dev_plans/scribe_tool_output_refinement/ARCHITECTURE_GUIDE.md", "byte_size": 73039, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 10, "line_count": 1887, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": ".scribe/docs/dev_plans/scribe_tool_output_refinement/ARCHITECTURE_GUIDE.md", "sha256": "403991fc49fbd3c1033a825e491b64a613745850f75763e759446be4929bd65e"}}; timestamp=2026-01-03T19:20:34.107622Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log
[ℹ️] [2026-01-03 19:20:44 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 32750, "byte_start": 29458, "content": "### Stage 6: DB Mode Activation (Production Rollout)\n**Duration:** 4 days + 48 hours monitoring\n\n**Tasks:**\n1. Set feature flag default to True (2 hours)\n   - `config/reminder_config.json`: Change `\"use_db_cooldown_tracking\": true`\n   - Commit with detailed changelog explaining migration\n\n2. Archive file-based cooldown cache (1 hour)\n   - Copy `data/reminder_cooldowns.json` to `data/reminder_cooldowns.json.archive`\n   - Add timestamp to archive filename\n   - Document archive purpose in commit message\n\n3. Run full test suite with DB mode (1 day)\n   - All 69 functional tests must pass\n   - All new reminder tests must pass\n   - Performance tests must validate <5ms query SLA\n\n4. Deploy to production (2 hours)\n   - Deploy to staging environment first\n   - Monitor for 4 hours in staging\n   - Deploy to production if staging clean\n\n5. Monitor production for 48 hours (passive)\n   - Watch error logs for reminder-related failures\n   - Monitor DB query performance (check <5ms SLA)\n   - Verify no regression in reminder delivery rate\n   - Be ready for immediate rollback if issues detected\n\n**Deliverables:**\n- [ ] Feature flag default changed to True\n- [ ] File-based cache archived\n- [ ] All tests passing in DB mode\n- [ ] Production deployment successful\n- [ ] 48-hour monitoring complete with no issues\n\n**Acceptance Criteria:**\n- [ ] All tests pass with `use_db_cooldown_tracking=True`\n- [ ] DB queries meet <5ms p95 SLA\n- [ ] No reminder delivery regressions\n- [ ] No increase in error rates\n- [ ] Session isolation working in production\n\n**Dependencies:** Stage 5 (all features implemented)\n\n**Risks:**\n- **High Risk:** Production issues require immediate rollback\n- **Mitigation:** Feature flag rollback available, archived file can be restored\n- **Rollback Plan:** Set `use_db_cooldown_tracking=false`, restore archived JSON file, redeploy\n\n---\n\n### Stage 7: Cleanup (Post-Validation)\n**Duration:** 2 days (after 2-week production validation)\n\n**Tasks:**\n1. Remove file-based cooldown code (1 day)\n   - Delete `_load_cooldown_cache()`, `_save_cooldown_cache()`, `_cleanup_cooldown_cache()` methods\n   - Remove `_cooldown_cache_path` attribute from ReminderEngine\n   - Remove legacy hash generation branch (keep only DB mode)\n   - Update class docstrings to reflect DB-only mode\n\n2. Remove feature flag (2 hours)\n   - Delete `\"use_db_cooldown_tracking\"` from `config/reminder_config.json`\n   - Remove conditional logic from `_get_reminder_hash()`\n   - Simplify to single hash format (session-aware only)\n\n3. Update documentation (4 hours)\n   - Update ARCHITECTURE_GUIDE.md to remove \"migration\" language\n   - Update tool documentation to reflect DB-backed reminder system\n   - Add session isolation behavior to README\n\n4. Final test suite run (2 hours)\n   - All tests pass with simplified code\n   - No feature flag references remain\n   - Code coverage maintained or improved\n\n**Deliverables:**\n- [ ] File-based code removed\n- [ ] Feature flag removed\n- [ ] Documentation updated\n- [ ] All tests passing\n\n**Acceptance Criteria:**\n- [ ] No file-based cooldown code remains\n- [ ] No feature flag references in codebase\n- [ ] Documentation reflects DB-only mode\n- [ ] All tests pass\n- [ ] Code is simpler and more maintainable\n\n**Dependencies:** Stage 6 + 2 weeks production validation\n", "line_end": 875, "line_start": 785}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/.scribe/docs/dev_plans/scribe_tool_output_refinement/PHASE_PLAN.md", "byte_size": 33848, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 5, "line_count": 908, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": ".scribe/docs/dev_plans/scribe_tool_output_refinement/PHASE_PLAN.md", "sha256": "12e129b1da59937e2cb25902060b37b885286651033abee04208d9baca4131b5"}}; timestamp=2026-01-03T19:20:44.961888Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log

Set feature flag default to True (2 hours)\\n   - `config/reminder_config.json`: Change `\\\"use_db_cooldown_tracking\\\": true`\\n   - Commit with detailed changelog explaining migration\\n\\n2. Archive file-based cooldown cache (1 hour)\\n   - Copy `data/reminder_cooldowns.json` to `data/reminder_cooldowns.json.archive`\\n   - Add timestamp to archive filename\\n   - Document archive purpose in commit message\\n\\n3. Run full test suite with DB mode (1 day)\\n   - All 69 functional tests must pass\\n   - All new reminder tests must pass\\n   - Performance tests must validate <5ms query SLA\\n\\n4. Deploy to production (2 hours)\\n   - Deploy to staging environment first\\n   - Monitor for 4 hours in staging\\n   - Deploy to production if staging clean\\n\\n5. Monitor production for 48 hours (passive)\\n   - Watch error logs for reminder-related failures\\n   - Monitor DB query performance (check <5ms SLA)\\n   - Verify no regression in reminder delivery rate\\n   - Be ready for immediate rollback if issues detected\\n\\n**Deliverables:**\\n- [ ] Feature flag default changed to True\\n- [ ] File-based cache archived\\n- [ ] All tests passing in DB mode\\n- [ ] Production deployment successful\\n- [ ] 48-hour monitoring complete with no issues\\n\\n**Acceptance Criteria:**\\n- [ ] All tests pass with `use_db_cooldown_tracking=True`\\n- [ ] DB queries meet <5ms p95 SLA\\n- [ ] No reminder delivery regressions\\n- [ ] No increase in error rates\\n- [ ] Session isolation working in production\\n\\n**Dependencies:** Stage 5 (all features implemented)\\n\\n**Risks:**\\n- **High Risk:** Production issues require immediate rollback\\n- **Mitigation:** Feature flag rollback available, archived file can be restored\\n- **Rollback Plan:** Set `use_db_cooldown_tracking=false`, restore archived JSON file, redeploy\\n\\n---\\n\\n### Stage 7: Cleanup (Post-Validation)\\n**Duration:** 2 days (after 2-week production validation)\\n\\n**Tasks:**\\n1. Remove file-based cooldown code (1 day)\\n   - Delete `_load_cooldown_cache()`, `_save_cooldown_cache()`, `_cleanup_cooldown_cache()` methods\\n   - Remove `_cooldown_cache_path` attribute from ReminderEngine\\n   - Remove legacy hash generation branch (keep only DB mode)\\n   - Update class docstrings to reflect DB-only mode\\n\\n2. Remove feature flag (2 hours)\\n   - Delete `\\\"use_db_cooldown_tracking\\\"` from `config/reminder_config.json`\\n   - Remove conditional logic from `_get_reminder_hash()`\\n   - Simplify to single hash format (session-aware only)\\n\\n3. Update documentation (4 hours)\\n   - Update ARCHITECTURE_GUIDE.md to remove \\\"migration\\\" language\\n   - Update tool documentation to reflect DB-backed reminder system\\n   - Add session isolation behavior to README\\n\\n4. Problem Statement\\n<!-- ID: problem_statement -->\\n## 1. Problem Statement\\n<!-- ID: problem_statement -->\\n- **Context:** Scribe MCP tools return JSON-wrapped outputs that are difficult for AI agents to read and use effectively. The `read_file` tool exemplifies this: content is buried in nested structures with escaped newlines, no line numbers, and metadata mixed with content. Agents avoid using these tools despite their audit capabilities.\\n- **Goals:**\\n  - Make ALL Scribe MCP tool outputs agent-friendly and highly readable\\n  - Preserve full audit trail (sha256, provenance, timestamps, agent identity)\\n  - Start with `read_file` as the priority tool, then expand to all tools\\n  - Create output formats that agents WANT to use\\n  - Maintain backward compatibility for existing integrations\\n- **Non-Goals:**\\n  - Breaking existing API contracts\\n  - Removing audit/provenance capabilities\\n  - Creating entirely new tools (we refine existing ones)\\n- **Success Metrics:**\\n  - Agents prefer Scribe tools over raw file operations\\n  - Output is readable at a glance (line numbers, actual line breaks)\\n  - Audit data is accessible but not obstructive\\n  - All existing tests continue to pass\\n<!-- ID: requirements_constraints -->\\n## 2. Requirements & Constraints\\n<!-- ID: requirements_constraints -->\\n\\n### Functional Requirements\\n\\n**FR1: Multi-Format Output Support**\\n- All tools MUST support three output formats: `readable`, `structured`, `compact`\\n- Format selection via optional `format` parameter (default: `structured` for backward compatibility)\\n- Each format serves distinct use case:\\n  - `readable`: Agent-friendly with line numbers, visual separators, actual line breaks\\n  - `structured`: Current JSON format for programmatic consumers (backward compatible)\\n  - `compact`: Minimal output for token-constrained scenarios\\n\\n**FR2: Readable Format Specification**\\n- Content MUST be line-numbered using cat -n style: `  1\\u2192content`, `  2\\u2192content`\\n- Line numbers right-aligned with arrow separator for scannability\\n- Actual line breaks (NOT escaped `\\\\n`)\\n- Metadata in separate collapsible section with visual boundaries\\n- ASCII art separators for clear visual hierarchy\\n\\n**FR3: Priority Tool Coverage**\\n1. `read_file` - File content with provenance (HIGHEST PRIORITY)\\n2. `append_entry` - Log confirmation\\n3. `read_recent` - Recent log entries\\n4. `query_entries` - Search results\\n5. `list_projects` - Project listing\\n6. All remaining 9 tools\\n\\n**FR4: Metadata Preservation**\\n- All audit data MUST be preserved: sha256, timestamps, provenance, agent identity\\n- Metadata placed in separate section (not mixed with content)\\n- Collapsible metadata blocks using ASCII separators\\n- Full audit trail accessible in all formats\\n\\n### Non-Functional Requirements\\n\\n**NFR1: Backward Compatibility**\\n- Default format MUST be `structured` (current behavior)\\n- Existing tool responses MUST remain unchanged when format parameter not specified\\n- All existing tests MUST pass without modification\\n- No breaking changes to tool APIs\\n\\n**NFR2: Performance**\\n- Output formatting MUST NOT degrade performance below existing baselines\\n- `readable` format generation \\u2264 5ms overhead per tool call\\n- Memory overhead \\u2264 2x current usage (for formatted strings)\\n- Existing performance test suite MUST pass\\n\\n**NFR3: Maintainability**\\n- Extend existing `utils/response.py` - NO new modules\\n- Single source of truth for formatting logic\\n- Consistent API across all tools\\n- Clear documentation for format implementations\\n\\n### Hard Constraints\\n\\n**C1: Audit Trail Completeness**\\n- SHA256 hashes for all file operations\\n- Timestamp precision to microsecond\\n- Agent identity tracking\\n- Execution context provenance\\n- All fields currently in responses MUST remain accessible\\n\\n**C2: Error Reporting Structure**\\n- `ok` boolean for success/failure\\n- `error` field with descriptive messages\\n- Error format consistent across all three output modes\\n- Stack traces preserved in structured format\\n\\n**C3: Reminders System Integration**\\n- Reminder arrays MUST be included in all responses\\n- Reminder format preserved for downstream processing\\n- Context-aware reminders based on project state\\n\\n**C4: Storage Backend Neutrality**\\n- Formatting layer MUST NOT depend on SQLite vs PostgreSQL\\n- Work with abstract storage interface only\\n- No database-specific output formatting\\n\\n**C5: MCP Protocol Compliance**\\n- All tools remain valid MCP tool definitions\\n- Parameter types compatible with MCP type system\\n- Response structure parseable by MCP clients\\n\\n### Assumptions\\n\\n- Filesystem read/write access for output operations\\n- Python 3.10+ runtime with typing support\\n- UTF-8 encoding for all text content\\n- Terminal width \\u2265 80 characters for readable format optimal display\\n\\n### Risks & Mitigations\\n\\n**R1: Performance Degradation**\\n- Risk: String formatting overhead for readable mode\\n- Mitigation: Lazy formatting (only when format='readable'), optimize string operations, performance benchmarks in CI\\n\\n**R2: Breaking Existing Consumers**\\n- Risk: Unknown external dependencies on current response structure\\n- Mitigation: Default format='structured', comprehensive testing, version documentation\\n\\n**R3: Format Inconsistency**\\n- Risk: Different tools implement readable format differently\\n- Mitigation: Centralized ResponseFormatter, comprehensive format specification, code review checklist\\n\\n**R4: Metadata Loss**\\n- Risk: Readable format might accidentally omit critical audit data\\n- Mitigation: Metadata preservation tests, audit trail validation, explicit metadata section in readable output\\n\\n**R5: Maintenance Burden**\\n- Risk: Three formats = 3x maintenance cost\\n- Mitigation: Shared formatting logic in ResponseFormatter, automated testing for all formats, format specification document\\n<!-- ID: architecture_overview -->\\n## 3. Architecture Overview\\n<!-- ID: architecture_overview -->\\n- **Solution Summary:** Introduce a unified output formatting layer for all Scribe MCP tools that separates audit metadata from readable content, with configurable format options.\\n- **Tools In Scope (Priority Order):**\\n  1. `read_file` - File reading with provenance (PRIORITY)\\n  2. `append_entry` - Log entry creation and confirmation\\n  3. `read_recent` - Recent log retrieval\\n  4. `query_entries` - Log search and filtering\\n  5. `manage_docs` - \"chunk_index\": 0, \"content\": \"\\n# \\ud83c\\udfd7\\ufe0f Architecture Guide \\u2014 scribe_tool_output_refinement\\n**Author:** Scribe\\n**Version:** Draft v0.1\\n**Status:** Draft\\n**Last Updated:** 2026-01-02 13:47:01 UTC\\n\\n> Architecture guide for scribe_tool_output_refinement.\\n\\n---\\n## 1. Problem Statement\\n<!-- ID: problem_statement -->\\n## 1. Problem Statement\\n<!-- ID: problem_statement -->\\n- **Context:** Scribe MCP tools return JSON-wrapped outputs that are difficult for AI agents to read and use effectively. The `read_file` tool exemplifies this: content is buried in nested structures with escaped newlines, no line numbers, and metadata mixed with content. Agents avoid using these tools despite their audit capabilities.\\n- **Goals:**\\n  - Make ALL Scribe MCP tool outputs agent-friendly and highly readable\\n  - Preserve full audit trail (sha256, provenance, timestamps, agent identity)\\n  - Start with `read_file` as the priority tool, then expand to all tools\\n  - Create output formats that agents WANT to use\\n  - Maintain backward compatibility for existing integrations\\n- **Non-Goals:**\\n  - Breaking existing API contracts\\n  - Removing audit/provenance capabilities\\n  - Creating entirely new tools (we refine existing ones)\\n- **Success Metrics:**\\n  - Agents prefer Scribe tools over raw file operations\\n  - Output is readable at a glance (line numbers, actual line breaks)\\n  - Audit data is accessible but not obstructive\\n  - All existing tests continue to pass\\n<!-- ID: requirements_constraints -->\\n## 2. Requirements & Constraints\\n<!-- ID: requirements_constraints -->\\n\\n### Functional Requirements\\n\\n**FR1: Multi-Format Output Support**\\n- All tools MUST support three output formats: `readable`, `structured`, `compact`\\n- Format selection via optional `format` parameter (default: `structured` for backward compatibility)\\n- Each format serves distinct use case:\\n  - `readable`: Agent-friendly with line numbers, visual separators, actual line breaks\\n  - `structured`: Current JSON format for programmatic consumers (backward compatible)\\n  - `compact`: Minimal output for token-constrained scenarios\\n\\n**FR2: Readable Format Specification**\\n- Content MUST be line-numbered using cat -n style: `  1\\u2192content`, `  2\\u2192content`\\n- Line numbers right-aligned with arrow separator for scannability\\n- Actual line breaks (NOT escaped `\\\\n`)\\n- Metadata in separate collapsible section with visual boundaries\\n- ASCII art separators for clear visual hierarchy\\n\\n**FR3: Priority Tool Coverage**\\n1. `read_file` - File content with provenance (HIGHEST PRIORITY)\\n2. `append_entry` - Log confirmation\\n3. `read_recent` - Recent log entries\\n4. `query_entries` - Search results\\n5. `list_projects` - Project listing\\n6. All remaining 9 tools\\n\\n**FR4: Metadata Preservation**\\n- All audit data MUST be preserved: sha256, timestamps, provenance, agent identity\\n- Metadata placed in separate section (not mixed with content)\\n- Collapsible metadata blocks using ASCII separators\\n- Full audit trail accessible in all formats\\n\\n### Non-Functional Requirements\\n\\n**NFR1: Backward Compatibility**\\n- Default format MUST be `structured` (current behavior)\\n- Existing tool responses MUST remain unchanged when format parameter not specified\\n- All existing tests MUST pass without modification\\n- No breaking changes to tool APIs\\n\\n**NFR2: Performance**\\n- Output formatting MUST NOT degrade performance below existing baselines\\n- `readable` format generation \\u2264 5ms overhead per tool call\\n- Memory overhead \\u2264 2x current usage (for formatted strings)\\n- Existing performance test suite MUST pass\\n\\n**NFR3: Maintainability**\\n- Extend existing `utils/response.py` - NO new modules\\n- Single source of truth for formatting logic\\n- Consistent API across all tools\\n- Clear documentation for format implementations\\n\\n### Hard Constraints\\n\\n**C1: Audit Trail Completeness**\\n- SHA256 hashes for all file operations\\n- Timestamp precision to microsecond\\n- Agent identity tracking\\n- Execution context provenance\\n- All fields currently in responses MUST remain accessible\\n\\n**C2: Error Reporting Structure**\\n- `ok` boolean for success/failure\\n- `error` field with descriptive messages\\n- Error format consistent across all three output modes\\n- Stack traces preserved in structured format\\n\\n**C3: Reminders System Integration**\\n- Reminder arrays MUST be included in all responses\\n- Reminder format preserved for downstream processing\\n- Context-aware reminders based on project state\\n\\n**C4: Storage Backend Neutrality**\\n- Formatting layer MUST NOT depend on SQLite vs PostgreSQL\\n- Work with abstract storage interface only\\n- No database-specific output formatting\\n\\n**C5: MCP Protocol Compliance**\\n- All tools remain valid MCP tool definitions\\n- Parameter types compatible with MCP type system\\n- Response structure parseable by MCP clients\\n\\n### Assumptions\\n\\n- Filesystem read/write access for output operations\\n- Python 3.10+ runtime with typing support\\n- UTF-8 encoding for all text content\\n- Terminal width \\u2265 80 characters for readable format optimal display\\n\\n### Risks & Mitigations\\n\\n**R1: Performance Degradation**\\n- Risk: String formatting overhead for readable mode\\n- Mitigation: Lazy formatting (only when format='readable'), optimize string operations, performance benchmarks in CI\\n\\n**R2: Breaking Existing Consumers**\\n- Risk: Unknown external dependencies on current response structure\\n- Mitigation: Default format='structured', comprehensive testing, version documentation\\n\\n**R3: Format Inconsistency**\\n- Risk: Different tools implement readable format differently\\n- Mitigation: Centralized ResponseFormatter, comprehensive format specification, code review checklist\\n\\n**R4: Metadata Loss**\\n- Risk: Readable format might accidentally omit critical audit data\\n- Mitigation: Metadata preservation tests, audit trail validation, explicit metadata section in readable output\\n\\n**R5: Maintenance Burden**\\n- Risk: Three formats = 3x maintenance cost\\n- Mitigation: Shared formatting logic in ResponseFormatter, automated testing for all formats, format specification document\\n<!-- ID: architecture_overview -->\\n## 3. Architecture Overview\\n<!-- ID: architecture_overview -->\\n- **Solution Summary:** Introduce a unified output formatting layer for all Scribe MCP tools that separates audit metadata from readable content, with configurable format options.\\n- **Tools In Scope (Priority Order):**\\n  1. `read_file` - File reading with provenance (PRIORITY)\\n  2. `append_entry` - Log entry creation and confirmation\\n  3. `read_recent` - Recent log retrieval\\n  4. `query_entries` - Log search and filtering\\n  5. `manage_docs` - Document management operations\\n  6. `list_projects` - Project discovery\\n  7. `get_project` - Project context\\n  8. `set_project` - Project initialization\\n  9. `rotate_log` - Log rotation\\n  10. `scribe_doctor` - Diagnostics\\n- **Proposed Output Structure (To Be Refined by Research):**\\n  ```\\n  \\u250c\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2510\\n  \\u2502 METADATA BLOCK (collapsible/optional)   \\u2502\\n  \\u2502 - sha256, provenance, timestamps        \\u2502\\n  \\u251c\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2524\\n  \\u2502 CONTENT BLOCK (primary, readable)       \\u2502\\n  \\u2502 - Line numbers, actual formatting       \\u2502\\n  \\u2502 - Clean, scannable output               \\u2502\\n  \\u2514\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2518\\n  ```\\n- **Format Options (To Be Validated):**\\n  - `readable` - Human/agent-friendly (default)\\n  - `structured` - Full JSON for programmatic use\\n  - `compact` - Minimal output for quick checks\\n- **Key Constraints:**\\n  - Must not break existing API consumers\\n  - Audit trail must remain complete\\n  - Performance must not degrade significantly\\n<!-- ID: detailed_design -->\\n## 4. Detailed Design\\n<!-- ID: detailed_design -->\\n\\n### 4.1 ResponseFormatter Extension Architecture\\n\\n**Core Design Pattern: Extend Existing Infrastructure**\\n\\n\", \"frontmatter_stripped\": or {},\\n        )\\n        return LoggingToolMixin.apply_context_payload(response, context)\\n\\n    @staticmethod\\n    def validate_metadata_requirements(\\n        log_definition: Dict[str, annotations\\n\\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nfrom scribe_mcp import server as server_module\\nfrom scribe_mcp.server import app\\nfrom scribe_mcp.tools.project_utils import load_active_project, load_project_config\\nfrom scribe_mcp.shared.base_logging_tool import LoggingToolMixin\\nfrom scribe_mcp.shared.logging_utils import LoggingContext, ProjectResolutionError\\nfrom scribe_mcp.shared.project_registry import ProjectRegistry\\nfrom scribe_mcp.shared.logging_utils import resolve_log_definition\\nfrom scribe_mcp.config import log_config as log_config_module\\nfrom scribe_mcp.utils.logs import parse_log_line, read_all_lines\\n\\n\\nclass _GetProjectHelper(LoggingToolMixin):\\n    def __init__(self) -> None:\\n        self.server_module = server_module\\n\\n\\n_GET_PROJECT_HELPER = _GetProjectHelper()\\n_PROJECT_REGISTRY = ProjectRegistry()\\n\\n\\nasync def _compute_doc_status(project_name: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\\n    info = _PROJECT_REGISTRY.get_project(project_name)\\n    if not info:\\n        return {}\\n    docs_meta = (info.meta or {}).get(\\\"docs\\\") or {}\\n    flags = docs_meta.get(\\\"flags\\\") or {}\\n    return {\\n        \\\"flags\\\": flags,\\n        \\\"baseline_hashes\\\": docs_meta.get(\\\"baseline_hashes\\\") or {},\\n        \\\"current_hashes\\\": docs_meta.get(\\\"current_hashes\\\") or {},\\n        \\\"last_update_at\\\": docs_meta.get(\\\"last_update_at\\\"),\\n        \\\"update_count\\\": docs_meta.get(\\\"update_count\\\"),\\n    }\\n\\n\\nasync def _count_log_entries(log_path) -> int:\\n    try:\\n        lines = await read_all_lines(log_path)\\n    except Exception:\\n        return 0\\n    count = 0\\n    for line in lines:\\n        if parse_log_line(line):\\n            count += 1\\n    return count\\n\\n\\nasync def _compute_log_counts(project: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\\n    counts: Dict[str, Any] = {}\\n    logs = log_config_module.load_log_config()\\n    for log_type in sorted(logs.keys()):\\n        try:\\n            path, _definition = resolve_log_definition(project, log_type)\\n            if not path.exists():\\n                counts[log_type] = 0\\n                continue\\n            counts[log_type] = await _count_log_entries(path)\\n        except Exception:\\n            continue\\n    return counts\\n\\n\\nasync def _read_recent_progress_entries(progress_log_path: str, limit: int = 5) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Read last N entries from progress log.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        progress_log_path: Path to PROGRESS_LOG.md file\\n        limit: Maximum number of recent entries to return (1-5)\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        List of entry dicts with timestamp, emoji, agent, message\\n        (COMPLETE messages - NO truncation!)\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not progress_log_path or not Path(progress_log_path).exists():\\n        return []\\n\\n    try:\\n        # Read the log file\\n        with open(progress_log_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f:\\n            lines = f.readlines()\\n\\n        # Parse entries (lines starting with '[')\\n        entries = []\\n        for line in lines:\\n            line = line.strip()\\n            if not line.startswith('['):\\n                continue\\n\\n            # Parse entry format: [emoji] [timestamp] [Agent: name] [Project: name] message\\n            # Example: [\\u2139\\ufe0f] [2026-01-03 09:53:42 UTC] [Agent: Orchestrator] [Project: xyz] Message here\\n\\n            try:\\n                parts = line.split('] ', 4)  # Split on '] ' up to 5 parts\\n                if len(parts) < 5:\\n                    continue\\n\\n                emoji = parts[0].strip('[')\\n                timestamp = parts[1].strip('[')\\n                agent_part = parts[2].strip('[')  # \\\"Agent: name\\\"\\n                # Skip project part (parts[3])\\n                message = parts[4]\\n\\n                # Extract agent name\\n                agent = agent_part.replace('Agent: ', '') if 'Agent:' in agent_part else 'unknown'\\n\\n                entries.append({\\n                    \\\"emoji\\\": emoji,\\n                    \\\"timestamp\\\": timestamp,\\n                    \\\"agent\\\": agent,\\n                    \\\"message\\\": message  # COMPLETE message - NO truncation!\\n                })\\n            except:\\n                continue\\n\\n        # Return last N entries\\n        return entries[-limit:] if len(entries) > limit else entries\\n\\n    except Exception:\\n        return []\\n\\n\\nasync def _gather_doc_info(project: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Gather document information for a project.\\n\\n    Returns dict with architecture/phase_plan/checklist/progress info.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    from utils.response import default_formatter\\n\\n    progress_log = project.get('progress_log', '')\\n    if not progress_log or not Path(progress_log).exists():\\n        return {}\\n\\n    dev_plan_dir = Path(progress_log).parent\\n    result = {}\\n\\n    # Check standard documents\\n    arch_file = dev_plan_dir / \\\"ARCHITECTURE_GUIDE.md\\\"\\n    if arch_file.exists():\\n        result[\\\"architecture\\\"] = {\\n            \\\"exists\\\": True,\\n            \\\"lines\\\": default_formatter._get_doc_line_count(arch_file)\\n        }\\n\\n    phase_file = dev_plan_dir / \\\"PHASE_PLAN.md\\\"\\n    if phase_file.exists():\\n        result[\\\"phase_plan\\\"] = {\\n            \\\"exists\\\": True,\\n            \\\"lines\\\": default_formatter._get_doc_line_count(phase_file)\\n        }\\n\\n    checklist_file = dev_plan_dir / \\\"CHECKLIST.md\\\"\\n    if checklist_file.exists():\\n        result[\\\"checklist\\\"] = {\\n            \\\"exists\\\": True,\\n            \\\"lines\\\": default_formatter._get_doc_line_count(checklist_file)\\n        }\\n\\n    # Progress log - count entries\\n    prog_file = Path(progress_log)\\n    if prog_file.exists():\\n        try:\\n            with open(prog_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f:\\n                content = f.read()\\n                entry_count = sum(1 for line in content.split('\\\\n') if line.strip().startswith('['))\\n            result[\\\"progress\\\"] = {\\\"exists\\\": True, \\\"entries\\\": entry_count}\\n        except:\\n            result[\\\"progress\\\"] = {\\\"exists\\\": True, \\\"entries\\\": 0}\\n\\n    return result\\n\\n\\n@app.tool()\\nasync def get_project(project: Optional[str] = None, format: str = \\\"structured\\\") -> Dict[str, Any]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Return the active project selection, resolving defaults when necessary.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        project: Optional project name to retrieve\\n        format: Output format - \\\"readable\\\" (human-friendly), \\\"structured\\\" (full JSON), \\\"compact\\\" (minimal)\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    state_snapshot = await server_module.state_manager.record_tool(\\\"get_project\\\")\\n    agent_identity = server_module.get_agent_identity()\\n    agent_id = None\\n    if agent_identity:\\n        agent_id = await agent_identity.get_or_create_agent_id()\\n\\n    try:\\n        context: LoggingContext = await _GET_PROJECT_HELPER.prepare_context(\\n            tool_name=\\\"get_project\\\",\\n            agent_id=agent_id,\\n            explicit_project=project,\\n            require_project=False,\\n\", \"line_end\": 200, \"line_start\": 1}], \"frontmatter\": {}, \"frontmatter_byte_count\": 0, for key, ts in entries.items():\\n                if not isinstance(key, str) or not isinstance(ts, str):\\n                    continue\\n                try:\\n                    dt = self.history.reminder_hashes.update(loaded)\\n        except Exception:\\n            return\\n\\n    def _save_cooldown_cache(self) -> None:\\n        behavior = self.config.get(\\\"behavior\\\", {}) encoding=\\\"utf-8\\\")\\n            tmp.replace(path)\\n            self._cooldown_cache_dirty = False\\n        except Exception:\\n            return\\n\\n    def _cleanup_cooldown_cache(self) ->        k: v for k, v in self.history.reminder_hashes.items()\\n            if v >= cutoff\\n        }\\n        if len(self.history.reminder_hashes) != before:\\n            self._cooldown_cache_dirty = True\\n\\n    def reset_cooldowns(self, *, project_root: str, agent_id: Optional[str] = None) -> int:\\n        prefix = f\\\"{project_root} \\\"\\n        if agent_id:\\n            prefix = self.history.reminder_hashes.pop(key, None)\\n        if keys:\\n            self._cooldown_cache_dirty = True\\n            self._save_cooldown_cache()\\n        return len(keys)\\n\\n    def 'use_session_aware_hashes', False)\\n        session_id = str(variables.get(\\\"session_id\\\") or \\\"\\\")\\n\\n        if use_session_hash and session_id:\\n            # Session-aware hash (new \\\"\\\"),\\n                str(variables.get(\\\"tool_name\\\") or \\\"\\\"),\\n                reminder_key\\n            ]\\n        else:\\n            # Legacy hash (backward compatible)\\n            parts = [\\n                str(variables.get(\\\"project_root\\\") or \\\"\\\"),\\n                str(variables.get(\\\"agent_id\\\") or \\\"\\\"),\\n                str(variables.get(\\\"tool_name\\\") or \\\"\\\"),\\n                reminder_key\\n            ]\\n\\n        return hashlib.md5(\\\" \\\".join(parts).encode()).hexdigest()\\n\\n    def _should_show_reminder(self, reminder: ReminderInstance, context: ReminderContext) -> bool:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Check if reminder should be shown based on rules.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n        # Tool suppression\\n        if context.tool_name in reminder.tools_suppressed:\\n            return False\\n\\n        # Cooldown check\\n        cooldown_minutes = reminder.cooldown_minutes\\n        if cooldown_minutes <= 0 and reminder.category == \\\"teaching\\\":\\n            cooldown_minutes = int(self.config.get(\\\"behavior\\\", {}).get(\\\"default_teaching_cooldown_minutes\\\", 10))\\n\\n        if cooldown_minutes > 0:\\n            reminder_hash = self._get_reminder_hash(reminder.key, reminder.variables)\\n            last_shown = self.history.reminder_hashes.get(reminder_hash)\\n            if last_shown:\\n                cooldown_cutoff = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(minutes=cooldown_minutes)\\n                if last_shown > cooldown_cutoff:\\n                    return self.history.teaching_sessions.get(session_key, 0)\\n            max_sessions = self.config.get(\\\"behavior\\\", {}).get(\\\"max_teaching_reminders_per_session\\\", 3)\\n            if sessions_used >= max_sessions:\\n                return False\\n\\n        return True\\n\\n    def _format_reminder(self, reminder: ReminderInstance, use_short: bool = True) -> ReminderInstance:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Apply variable substitution and formatting to reminder.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        # Choose template\\n        template_key = \\\"short_template\\\" if use_short and \\\"short_template\\\" in = template.format(**reminder.variables)\\n            if reminder.context:\\n                formatted_context = reminder.context.format(**reminder.variables)\\n                reminder.context = formatted_context\\n        except KeyError as e:\\n            # Fallback to original template if variable missing\\n            formatted_message = reminder.message\\n\\n        reminder.message =       elif condition.startswith(\\\"scaffold=\\\"):\\n            expected = condition.split(\\\"=\\\")[1].strip().lower()\\n            actual = context.variables.get(\\\"scaffold\\\")\\n            if isinstance(actual, bool):\\n                actual_value = \\\"true\\\" if actual else \\\"false\\\"\\n            else:\\n                actual_value = str(actual).strip().lower()\\n Backward compatibility with flags OFF\n\nRun with: pytest tests/test_db_activation.py -v\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pytest\nimport asyncio\nimport time\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Dict, Any\n\n# Test imports\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path as P\nsys.path.insert(0, str(P(__file__).parent.parent))\n\nfrom scribe_mcp.storage.sqlite import SQLiteStorage\nfrom scribe_mcp.utils.reminder_engine import ReminderEngine, ReminderContext\nfrom utils.reminder_monitoring import validate_db_performance, validate_session_isolation\n\n# Get project root dynamically\nSCRIBE_ROOT = P(__file__).parent.parent\n\n\n# Fixtures\n@pytest.fixture\nasync def storage():\n    \"\"\"Provide clean storage instance for each test.\"\"\"\n    db_path = SCRIBE_ROOT / \".scribe\" / \"data\" / \"scribe.db\"\n    storage = SQLiteStorage(str(db_path))\n    await storage.setup()\n\n    # Clean test data before test\n    await storage.execute(\n        \"DELETE FROM scribe_reminder_history WHERE session_id LIKE 'test_%' OR project_name = 'test_activation'\"\n    )\n\n    yield storage\n\n    # Clean test data after test\n    await storage.execute(\n        \"DELETE FROM scribe_reminder_history WHERE session_id LIKE 'test_%' OR project_name = 'test_activation'\"\n    )\n\n    # Storage cleanup handled automatically\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\nasync def reminder_engine(storage):\n    \"\"\"Provide reminder engine instance.\"\"\"\n    return ReminderEngine(storage=storage)\n\n\n# Test 1: DB Mode Performance Benchmark\n@pytest.mark.asyncio\nasync def test_db_mode_performance_benchmark():\n    \"\"\"\n    Validate <5ms query SLA for DB operations.\n\n    Tests:\n    - record_reminder_shown() p95 <5ms\n    - check_reminder_cooldown() p95 <5ms\n    - cleanup_old_reminders() max <100ms\n\n    Failure indicates performance regression requiring optimization.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\ud83d\udd0d Running performance benchmark...\")\n\n    results = await validate_db_performance()\n\n    # Check overall success\n    assert results[\"ok\"], f\"Performance validation failed: {results.get('error', 'SLA violations')}\"\n\n    # Check SLA compliance\n    assert results[\"sla_compliance\"][\"passed\"], f\"SLA violations detected: {results['sla_compliance']['failed_operations']}\"\n\n    # Detailed assertions for each operation\n    record_perf = results[\"operations\"][\"record_reminder\"]\n    assert record_perf[\"p95_ms\"] < 5.0, f\"record_reminder p95 ({record_perf['p95_ms']:.2f}ms) exceeds 5ms SLA\"\n    assert record_perf[\"avg_ms\"] < 5.0, f\"record_reminder average ({record_perf['avg_ms']:.2f}ms) exceeds 5ms\"\n\n    cooldown_perf = results[\"operations\"][\"check_cooldown\"]\n    assert cooldown_perf[\"p95_ms\"] < 5.0, f\"check_cooldown p95 ({cooldown_perf['p95_ms']:.2f}ms) exceeds 5ms SLA\"\n    assert cooldown_perf[\"avg_ms\"] < 5.0, f\"check_cooldown average ({cooldown_perf['avg_ms']:.2f}ms) exceeds 5ms\"\n\n    cleanup_perf = results[\"operations\"][\"cleanup\"]\n    assert cleanup_perf[\"max_ms\"] < 100.0, f\"cleanup max ({cleanup_perf['max_ms']:.2f}ms) exceeds 100ms SLA\"\n\n    print(f\"\u2705 Performance SLA met:\")\n    print(f\"   - record_reminder: p95={record_perf['p95_ms']:.2f}ms avg={record_perf['avg_ms']:.2f}ms\")\n    print(f\"   - check_cooldown: p95={cooldown_perf['p95_ms']:.2f}ms avg={cooldown_perf['avg_ms']:.2f}ms\")\n    print(f\"   - cleanup: max={cleanup_perf['max_ms']:.2f}ms\")\n\n\n# Test 2: Session Isolation with Flags On\n@pytest.mark.asyncio\nasync def test_session_isolation_with_flags_on(storage, reminder_engine):\n    \"\"\"\n    Verify session isolation works when use_session_aware_hashes=True.\n\n    Tests:\n    - Different sessions generate different hashes for same reminder\n    - Cooldown tracking is session-isolated\n    - Session reset clears session-specific cooldowns\n\n    Note: This test validates the infrastructure exists, even if flags are OFF by default.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\ud83d\udd0d Testing session isolation infrastructure...\")\n\n    # Create two different session contexts\n    context1 = ReminderContext(\n        session_id=\"test_session_alpha\",\n        project_name=\"test_activation\",\n        operation_status=\"success\",\n        active_project_selected=True,\n        reminder_types=[\"teaching\"]\n    )\n\n    context2 = ReminderContext(\n        session_id=\"test_session_beta\",\n        project_name=\"test_activation\",\n        operation_status=\"success\",\n        active_project_selected=True,\n        reminder_types=[\"teaching\"]\n    )\n\n    # Generate hashes for same reminder in different sessions\n    reminder_text = \"Remember to check docs\"\n\n    hash1 = await reminder_engine._get_reminder_hash(\"teaching\", reminder_text, context1)\n    hash2 = await reminder_engine._get_reminder_hash(\"teaching\", reminder_text, context2)\n\n    # Record reminder for session 1\n    await storage.record_reminder_shown(\n        session_id=\"test_session_alpha\",\n        reminder_hash=hash1,\n        reminder_type=\"teaching\",\n        project_name=\"test_activation\",\n        operation_status=\"success\"\n    )\n\n    # Check cooldown for session 1 (should be in cooldown)\n    cooldown1 = await storage.check_reminder_cooldown(\n        session_id=\"test_session_alpha\",\n        reminder_hash=hash1,\n        cooldown_minutes=45\n    )\n\n    # Check cooldown for session 2 with different hash (should NOT be in cooldown)\n    cooldown2 = await storage.check_reminder_cooldown(\n        session_id=\"test_session_beta\",\n        reminder_hash=hash2,\n        cooldown_minutes=45\n    )\n\n    # Assertions - cooldown should be session-isolated\n    assert cooldown1, \"Session 1 should be in cooldown after showing reminder\"\n    assert not cooldown2, \"Session 2 should NOT be in cooldown (different session)\"\n\n    print(f\"\u2705 Session isolation working:\")\n    print(f\"   - (in cooldown: {cooldown1})\")\n    print(f\"   - Session 2 hash: {hash2[:16]}... (in cooldown: {cooldown2})\")\n    print(f\"   - Cooldowns are session-isolated: PASS\")\n\n\n# Test 3: DB Cooldown Tracking Functional\n@pytest.mark.asyncio\nasync def test_db_cooldown_tracking_functional(storage):\n    \"\"\"\n    Verify DB cooldown tracking works end-to-end.\n\n    Tests:\n    - record_reminder_shown() creates DB record\n    - check_reminder_cooldown() respects cooldown window\n    - Cooldown expires after configured time\n    - cleanup_old_reminders() removes old records\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\ud83d\udd0d Testing DB cooldown tracking functionality...\")\n\n    session_id = \"test_session_functional\"\n    reminder_hash = \"test_hash_functional_abc123\"\n\n    # Record reminder\n    await storage.record_reminder_shown(\n        session_id=session_id,\n        reminder_hash=reminder_hash,\n        reminder_type=\"teaching\",\n        project_name=\"test_activation\",\n        operation_status=\"success\"\n    )\n\n", "line_end": 200, "line_start": 1}, {"byte_end": 14291, "byte_start": 6888, "chunk_index": 1, "content": "    # Check cooldown immediately (should be in cooldown)\n    in_cooldown = await storage.check_reminder_cooldown(\n        session_id=session_id,\n        reminder_hash=reminder_hash,\n        cooldown_minutes=45\n    )\n    assert in_cooldown, \"Reminder should be in cooldown immediately after showing\"\n\n    # Check cooldown with very short window (should NOT be in cooldown)\n    not_in_cooldown = await storage.check_reminder_cooldown(\n        session_id=session_id,\n        reminder_hash=reminder_hash,\n        cooldown_minutes=0  # 0-minute cooldown = always allowed\n    )\n    assert not not_in_cooldown, \"With 0-minute cooldown, should always be allowed\"\n\n    # Verify record was created in DB\n    result = await storage.fetch_one(\n        \"SELECT * FROM scribe_reminder_history WHERE session_id = ? Verify recent record still exists\n    recent_result = await storage.fetch_one(\n        \"SELECT * FROM scribe_reminder_history WHERE session_id = ?\",\n        (session_id,)\n    )\n    assert recent_result is not None, \"Recent record should NOT be cleaned up\"\n\n    print(f\"\u2705 DB cooldown tracking functional:\")\n    print(f\"   - record_reminder_shown: PASS\")\n    print(f\"   - check_reminder_cooldown: PASS\")\n    print(f\"   - cleanup_old_reminders: PASS\")\n\n\n# Test 4: Feature Flags Toggle Safely\n@pytest.mark.asyncio\nasync def test_feature_flags_toggle(storage, reminder_engine):\n    \"\"\"\n    Verify feature flags can be enabled/disabled without breaking system.\n\n    Tests:\n    - System works with use_db_cooldown_tracking=False (file mode)\n    - System works with use_db_cooldown_tracking=True (DB mode)\n    - Toggling flags doesn't corrupt data\n    - Both modes can coexist (dual-mode support)\n\n    Note: This test validates the infrastructure, not the actual config file toggle.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\ud83d\udd0d Testing feature flag toggle safety...\")\n\n    # Verify DB record exists\n    db_record = await storage.fetch_one(\n        \"SELECT * FROM scribe_reminder_history WHERE session_id = ?\",\n        (session_id_db,)\n    )\n    assert db_record is not None, \"DB record should exist\"\n\n    # Should NOT show (we just recorded it)\n    assert not should_show, \"Reminder should not show (in cooldown)\"\n\n    # Verify legacy infrastructure doesn't break\n    # (File-based code still exists in ReminderEngine for fallback)\n    assert hasattr(reminder_engine, '_cooldown_cache_path'), \"Legacy file infrastructure should exist\"\n\n    print(f\"\u2705 Backward compatibility validated:\")\n    print(f\"   - DB infrastructure works: PASS\")\n    print(f\"   - ReminderEngine functional: PASS\")\n    print(f\"   - Legacy infrastructure intact: PASS\")\n    print(f\"   - No regressions: PASS\")\n\n\n# Test 6 (BONUS): Teaching Reminders Session Limit\n@pytest.mark.asyncio\nasync def test_teaching_reminders_session_limit(storage, reminder_engine):\n", "line_end": 400, "line_start": 201}, {"byte_end": 17188, "byte_start": 14291, "chunk_index": 2, "content": "    \"\"\"\n    BONUS: Verify teaching reminders limited to 3 per session.\n\n    Tests:\n    - Teaching reminder counter increments per session\n    - Limit enforced at 3 reminders per session\n    - Different sessions have independent counters\n    - Session reset clears teaching counter\n\n    This validates session-aware teaching reminder limits work.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\ud83d\udd0d BONUS: Testing teaching reminder session limits...\")\n\n    session_id = \"test_teaching_limit_session\"\n\n    # Create context for teaching reminders\n    context = ReminderContext(\n        session_id=session_id,\n        project_name=\"test_activation\",\n        operation_status=\"success\",\n        active_project_selected=True,\n        reminder_types=[\"teaching\"]\n    )\n\n    # Show 3 different teaching reminders (should all show)\n    for i in range(3):\n        reminder_hash = await reminder_engine._get_reminder_hash(\"teaching\", f\"Teaching {i}\", context)\n\n        should_show = await reminder_engine._should_show_reminder(\n            reminder_type=\"teaching\",\n            reminder_hash=reminder_hash,\n            context=context\n        )\n\n        assert should_show, f\"Teaching reminder {i+1}/3 should show\"\n\n        # Record it as shown\n        await storage.record_reminder_shown(\n            session_id=session_id,\n            reminder_hash=reminder_hash,\n            reminder_type=\"teaching\",\n            project_name=\"test_activation\",\n            operation_status=\"success\"\n        )\n\n    # Try 4th teaching reminder (should NOT show - limit reached)\n    reminder_hash_4 = await reminder_engine._get_reminder_hash(\"teaching\", \"Teaching 4\", context)\n\n    should_show_4 = await reminder_engine._should_show_reminder(\n        reminder_type=\"teaching\",\n        reminder_hash=reminder_hash_4,\n        context=context\n    )\n\n    assert not should_show_4, \"4th teaching reminder should NOT show (limit reached)\"\n\n    #
