[ℹ️] [2026-01-04 00:22:47 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 44898, "byte_start": 40160, "content": "                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS reminder_history (\n                        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n                        session_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n                        reminder_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n                        project_root TEXT,\n                        agent_id TEXT,\n                        tool_name TEXT,\n                        reminder_key TEXT,\n                        shown_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                        operation_status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'neutral' CHECK (operation_status IN ('success', 'failure', 'neutral')),\n                        context_metadata TEXT,\n                        FOREIGN KEY (session_id) REFERENCES scribe_sessions(session_id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n                    );\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_reminder_history_session_hash\n                        ON reminder_history(session_id, reminder_hash);\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_reminder_history_shown_at\n                        ON reminder_history(shown_at);\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_reminder_history_session_tool\n                        ON reminder_history(session_id, tool_name);\n                    \"\"\",\n                    # Document Management 2.0 Indexes\n                    \"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_document_sections_project ON document_sections(project_id);\",\n                    \"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_document_sections_updated ON document_sections(updated_at);\",\n                    \"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_document_changes_project ON document_changes(project_id);\",\n                    \"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_document_changes_created ON document_changes(created_at);\",\n                    \"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_sync_status_project ON sync_status(project_id);\",\n                    \"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_sync_status_status ON sync_status(sync_status);\",\n                    # Full-text search for document content\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS document_sections_fts\n                    USING fts5(document_type, section_id, content, content=document_sections, content_rowid=id)\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS document_sections_fts_insert\n                    AFTER INSERT ON document_sections BEGIN\n                        INSERT INTO document_sections_fts(rowid, document_type, section_id, content)\n                        VALUES (new.id, new.document_type, new.section_id, new.content);\n                    END\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS document_sections_fts_delete\n                    AFTER DELETE ON document_sections BEGIN\n                        INSERT INTO document_sections_fts(document_sections_fts, rowid, document_type, section_id, content)\n                        VALUES ('delete', old.id, old.document_type, old.section_id, old.content);\n                    END\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS document_sections_fts_update\n                    AFTER UPDATE ON document_sections BEGIN\n                        INSERT INTO document_sections_fts(document_sections_fts, rowid, document_type, section_id, content)\n                        VALUES ('delete', old.id, old.document_type, old.section_id, old.content);\n                        INSERT INTO document_sections_fts(rowid, document_type, section_id, content)\n                        VALUES (new.id, new.document_type, new.section_id, new.content);\n                    END\n                    \"\"\",\n                ]\n            )\n            # Ensure legacy databases have the newer scribe_projects columns\n            await self._ensure_column(\"scribe_projects\", \"repo_root\", \"TEXT\")\n            await self._ensure_column(\"scribe_projects\", \"progress_log_path\", \"TEXT\")\n            await self._ensure_column(\"scribe_projects\", \"status\", \"TEXT DEFAULT 'planning'\")\n            await self._ensure_column(\"scribe_projects\", \"phase\", \"TEXT DEFAULT 'setup'\")\n            await self._ensure_column(\"scribe_projects\", \"confidence\", \"REAL DEFAULT 0.0\")\n            await self._ensure_column(\"scribe_projects\", \"completed_at\", \"TIMESTAMP\")\n            await self._ensure_column(\"scribe_projects\", \"last_activity\", \"TIMESTAMP\")\n            await self._ensure_column(\"scribe_projects\", \"description\", \"TEXT\")\n            await self._ensure_column(\"scribe_projects\", \"last_entry_at\", \"TEXT\")\n", "line_end": 1050, "line_start": 978}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/storage/sqlite.py", "byte_size": 83379, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 10, "line_count": 1954, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "storage/sqlite.py", "sha256": "f5dc5e549cee4838ba5ad60acc19adf4a00d2b70e2566bb7f95ee45169b7695d"}}; timestamp=2026-01-04T00:22:47.345549Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log
[ℹ️] [2026-01-04 00:22:57 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 82028, "byte_start": 77905, "content": "    async def record_reminder_shown(\n        self,\n        session_id: str,\n        reminder_hash: str,\n        project_root: Optional[str] = None,\n        agent_id: Optional[str] = None,\n        tool_name: Optional[str] = None,\n        reminder_key: Optional[str] = None,\n        operation_status: str = \"neutral\",\n        context_metadata: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Record that a reminder was shown in a session (inserts new record).\n\n        Args:\n            session_id: Session identifier (FK to scribe_sessions)\n            reminder_hash: Hash identifying the reminder\n            project_root: Optional project root path\n            agent_id: Optional agent identifier\n            tool_name: Optional tool name (e.g., 'append_entry')\n            reminder_key: Optional reminder key (e.g., 'log_warning')\n            operation_status: Operation status ('success', 'failure', 'neutral')\n            context_metadata: Optional context metadata as dict\n        \"\"\"\n        # Performance monitoring (Stage 6)\n        start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n        await self._initialise()\n        async with self._write_lock:\n            context_json = json.dumps(context_metadata or {}, sort_keys=True)\n            await self._execute(\n                \"\"\"\n                INSERT INTO reminder_history\n                (session_id, reminder_hash, project_root, agent_id, tool_name,\n                 reminder_key, shown_at, operation_status, context_metadata)\n                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, ?, ?);\n                \"\"\",\n                (\n                    session_id,\n                    reminder_hash,\n                    project_root,\n                    agent_id,\n                    tool_name,\n                    reminder_key,\n                    operation_status,\n                    context_json,\n                ),\n            )\n\n        # Log slow queries (Stage 6)\n        elapsed_ms = (time.perf_counter() - start_time) * 1000\n        if elapsed_ms > SLOW_QUERY_THRESHOLD_MS:\n            logger.warning(\n                f\"Slow reminder query: record_reminder_shown took {elapsed_ms:.2f}ms \"\n                f\"(threshold: {SLOW_QUERY_THRESHOLD_MS}ms) [session={session_id[:16]}...]\"\n            )\n\n    async def check_reminder_cooldown(\n        self,\n        session_id: str,\n        reminder_hash: str,\n        cooldown_minutes: int = 15,\n    ) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if a reminder is within its cooldown period for a session.\n\n        Args:\n            session_id: Session identifier\n            reminder_hash: Hash identifying the reminder\n            cooldown_minutes: Cooldown period in minutes (default: 15)\n\n        Returns:\n            True if reminder was shown within cooldown window (suppress),\n            False if reminder can be shown (cooldown expired or never shown)\n        \"\"\"\n        # Performance monitoring (Stage 6)\n        start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n        await self._initialise()\n        cutoff_time = f\"datetime('now', '-{cooldown_minutes} minutes')\"\n\n        row = await self._fetchone(\n            f\"\"\"\n            SELECT COUNT(*) as count\n            FROM reminder_history\n            WHERE session_id = ?\n              AND reminder_hash = ?\n              AND shown_at > {cutoff_time};\n            \"\"\",\n            (session_id, reminder_hash),\n        )\n\n        result = (row[\"count\"] if row else 0) > 0\n\n        # Log slow queries (Stage 6)\n        elapsed_ms = (time.perf_counter() - start_time) * 1000\n        if elapsed_ms > SLOW_QUERY_THRESHOLD_MS:\n            logger.warning(\n                f\"Slow reminder query: check_reminder_cooldown took {elapsed_ms:.2f}ms \"\n                f\"(threshold: {SLOW_QUERY_THRESHOLD_MS}ms) [session={session_id[:16]}...]\"\n            )\n\n        return result\n\n    async def cleanup_reminder_history(\n        self,\n        cutoff_hours: int = 168,\n    ) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Delete old reminder history entries.\n\n        Args:\n            cutoff_hours: Delete entries older than this (default: 168 = 7 days)\n\n        Returns:\n            Number of entries deleted\n        \"\"\"\n", "line_end": 1920, "line_start": 1807}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/storage/sqlite.py", "byte_size": 83379, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 10, "line_count": 1954, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "storage/sqlite.py", "sha256": "f5dc5e549cee4838ba5ad60acc19adf4a00d2b70e2566bb7f95ee45169b7695d"}}; timestamp=2026-01-04T00:22:57.396815Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log
[ℹ️] [2026-01-04 00:23:07 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 82962, "byte_start": 82016, "content": "        \"\"\"\n        # Performance monitoring (Stage 6)\n        start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n        await self._initialise()\n        async with self._write_lock:\n            # Use direct connection since _execute doesn't return cursor\n            def _cleanup_sync():\n                conn = self._connect()\n                try:\n                    cursor = conn.execute(\n                        \"\"\"\n                        DELETE FROM reminder_history\n                        WHERE shown_at < datetime('now', ?    ' hours');\n                        \"\"\",\n                        (f\"-{cutoff_hours}\",),\n                    )\n                    deleted = cursor.rowcount\n                    conn.commit()\n                    return deleted\n                finally:\n                    conn.close()\n\n            result = await asyncio.to_thread(_cleanup_sync)\n\n        # Log slow queries (Stage 6) - cleanup has higher threshold (100ms vs 5ms)\n", "line_end": 1945, "line_start": 1920}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/storage/sqlite.py", "byte_size": 83379, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 10, "line_count": 1954, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "storage/sqlite.py", "sha256": "f5dc5e549cee4838ba5ad60acc19adf4a00d2b70e2566bb7f95ee45169b7695d"}}; timestamp=2026-01-04T00:23:07.393271Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log
[ℹ️] [2026-01-04 00:24:06 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 3799, "byte_start": 1946, "content": "# Test 1: DB Mode Performance Benchmark\n@pytest.mark.asyncio\nasync def test_db_mode_performance_benchmark():\n    \"\"\"\n    Validate <5ms query SLA for DB operations.\n\n    Tests:\n    - record_reminder_shown() p95 <5ms\n    - check_reminder_cooldown() p95 <5ms\n    - cleanup_reminder_history() max <100ms\n\n    Failure indicates performance regression requiring optimization.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"\\n\ud83d\udd0d Running performance benchmark...\")\n\n    results = await validate_db_performance()\n\n    # Check overall success\n    assert results[\"ok\"], f\"Performance validation failed: {results.get('error', 'SLA violations')}\"\n\n    # Check SLA compliance\n    assert results[\"sla_compliance\"][\"passed\"], f\"SLA violations detected: {results['sla_compliance']['failed_operations']}\"\n\n    # Detailed assertions for each operation\n    record_perf = results[\"operations\"][\"record_reminder\"]\n    assert record_perf[\"p95_ms\"] < 5.0, f\"record_reminder p95 ({record_perf['p95_ms']:.2f}ms) exceeds 5ms SLA\"\n    assert record_perf[\"avg_ms\"] < 5.0, f\"record_reminder average ({record_perf['avg_ms']:.2f}ms) exceeds 5ms\"\n\n    cooldown_perf = results[\"operations\"][\"check_cooldown\"]\n    assert cooldown_perf[\"p95_ms\"] < 5.0, f\"check_cooldown p95 ({cooldown_perf['p95_ms']:.2f}ms) exceeds 5ms SLA\"\n    assert cooldown_perf[\"avg_ms\"] < 5.0, f\"check_cooldown average ({cooldown_perf['avg_ms']:.2f}ms) exceeds 5ms\"\n\n    cleanup_perf = results[\"operations\"][\"cleanup\"]\n    assert cleanup_perf[\"max_ms\"] < 100.0, f\"cleanup max ({cleanup_perf['max_ms']:.2f}ms) exceeds 100ms SLA\"\n\n    print(f\"\u2705 Performance SLA met:\")\n    print(f\"   - record_reminder: p95={record_perf['p95_ms']:.2f}ms avg={record_perf['avg_ms']:.2f}ms\")\n    print(f\"   - check_cooldown: p95={cooldown_perf['p95_ms']:.2f}ms avg={cooldown_perf['avg_ms']:.2f}ms\")\n    print(f\"   - cleanup: max={cleanup_perf['max_ms']:.2f}ms\")\n", "line_end": 113, "line_start": 75}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/tests/test_db_activation.py", "byte_size": 18763, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 3, "line_count": 519, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "tests/test_db_activation.py", "sha256": "5fe39c698d7ed669a0fee7890af56ee2af3a1670031d5df8fe283c53a2a7a55f"}}; timestamp=2026-01-04T00:24:06.057996Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log
[ℹ️] [2026-01-04 00:24:19 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 5585, "byte_start": 0, "content": "\"\"\"\nReminder System Monitoring and Validation Utilities (Stage 6)\n\nProvides diagnostic and validation functions for DB-backed reminder tracking:\n- Performance benchmarking (validate <5ms SLA)\n- Session isolation verification\n- Reminder statistics and health checks\n\nUsage:\n    from utils.reminder_monitoring import validate_db_performance, validate_session_isolation\n\n    # Check performance\n    results = await validate_db_performance()\n\n    # Verify session isolation\n    isolation_check = await validate_session_isolation()\n\n    # Get statistics\n    stats = await get_reminder_statistics()\n\"\"\"\n\nimport asyncio\nimport time\nfrom typing import Dict, Any, List\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom scribe_mcp.storage.sqlite import SQLiteStorage\n\n# Get project root dynamically\nSCRIBE_ROOT = Path(__file__).parent.parent\n\n\nasync def validate_db_performance() -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Benchmark reminder DB queries to ensure <5ms SLA.\n\n    Tests all critical reminder operations:\n    - record_reminder_shown() performance\n    - check_reminder_cooldown() performance\n    - cleanup_reminder_history() performance\n\n    Returns:\n        Dict with performance metrics:\n        {\n            \"ok\": True/False,\n            \"operations\": {\n                \"record_reminder\": {\"avg_ms\": X, \"max_ms\": Y, \"min_ms\": Z, \"p95_ms\": W},\n                \"check_cooldown\": {...},\n                \"cleanup\": {...}\n            },\n            \"sla_compliance\": {\"passed\": True/False, \"failed_operations\": []},\n            \"timestamp\": \"2026-01-03T12:00:00Z\"\n        }\n\n    SLA: p95 latency <5ms for record/check operations, <100ms for cleanup\n    \"\"\"\n    results = {\n        \"ok\": True,\n        \"operations\": {},\n        \"sla_compliance\": {\"passed\": True, \"failed_operations\": []},\n        \"timestamp\": datetime.utcnow().isoformat() + \"Z\"\n    }\n\n    try:\n        # Initialize storage\n        storage = SQLiteStorage(str(SCRIBE_ROOT / \".scribe\" / \"data\" / \"scribe.db\"))\n        await storage.setup()\n\n        # Benchmark record_reminder_shown (100 iterations)\n        record_times: List[float] = []\n        for i in range(100):\n            # Create session first (required for FK constraint)\n            await storage._execute(\n                \"INSERT OR IGNORE INTO scribe_sessions (session_id, started_at) VALUES (?, datetime('now'))\",\n                (f\"test_session_{i}\",)\n            )\n\n            start = time.perf_counter()\n            await storage.record_reminder_shown(\n                session_id=f\"test_session_{i}\",\n                reminder_hash=f\"test_hash_{i}\",\n                reminder_key=\"teaching\",\n                operation_status=\"success\"\n            )\n            elapsed = (time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000  # Convert to ms\n            record_times.append(elapsed)\n\n        # Benchmark check_reminder_cooldown (100 iterations)\n        cooldown_times: List[float] = []\n        for i in range(100):\n            start = time.perf_counter()\n            await storage.check_reminder_cooldown(\n                session_id=f\"test_session_{i}\",\n                reminder_hash=f\"test_hash_{i}\",\n                cooldown_minutes=45\n            )\n            elapsed = (time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000\n            cooldown_times.append(elapsed)\n\n        # Benchmark cleanup_reminder_history (1 iteration)\n        cleanup_times: List[float] = []\n        start = time.perf_counter()\n        await storage.cleanup_reminder_history(cutoff_hours=90*24)  # 90 days = 2160 hours\n        elapsed = (time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000\n        cleanup_times.append(elapsed)\n\n        # Calculate statistics\n        def calc_stats(times: List[float]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n            times_sorted = sorted(times)\n            return {\n                \"avg_ms\": sum(times) / len(times),\n                \"max_ms\": max(times),\n                \"min_ms\": min(times),\n                \"p95_ms\": times_sorted[int(len(times) * 0.95)] if times else 0,\n                \"p99_ms\": times_sorted[int(len(times) * 0.99)] if times else 0\n            }\n\n        results[\"operations\"][\"record_reminder\"] = calc_stats(record_times)\n        results[\"operations\"][\"check_cooldown\"] = calc_stats(cooldown_times)\n        results[\"operations\"][\"cleanup\"] = calc_stats(cleanup_times)\n\n        # Check SLA compliance\n        sla_threshold = 5.0  # 5ms for record/check operations\n        cleanup_threshold = 100.0  # 100ms for cleanup\n\n        if results[\"operations\"][\"record_reminder\"][\"p95_ms\"] > sla_threshold:\n            results[\"sla_compliance\"][\"passed\"] = False\n            results[\"sla_compliance\"][\"failed_operations\"].append({\n                \"operation\": \"record_reminder\",\n                \"p95_ms\": results[\"operations\"][\"record_reminder\"][\"p95_ms\"],\n                \"threshold_ms\": sla_threshold\n            })\n\n        if results[\"operations\"][\"check_cooldown\"][\"p95_ms\"] > sla_threshold:\n            results[\"sla_compliance\"][\"passed\"] = False\n            results[\"sla_compliance\"][\"failed_operations\"].append({\n                \"operation\": \"check_cooldown\",\n                \"p95_ms\": results[\"operations\"][\"check_cooldown\"][\"p95_ms\"],\n                \"threshold_ms\": sla_threshold\n            })\n\n        if results[\"operations\"][\"cleanup\"][\"max_ms\"] > cleanup_threshold:\n            results[\"sla_compliance\"][\"passed\"] = False\n            results[\"sla_compliance\"][\"failed_operations\"].append({\n                \"operation\": \"cleanup\",\n                \"max_ms\": results[\"operations\"][\"cleanup\"][\"max_ms\"],\n
